- **kit-setup**: Scaffold this workspace for the kit from inside a session: runs setup.sh --personal, reports, hands over to /kit-health. Use once, when setting up this workspace right after plugin install. ([MdaaaaO/ai-baton](https://github.com/MdaaaaO/ai-baton/tree/HEAD/skills/kit-setup))

- **kit-skip-review**: Record a skip of the kit's final-review Stop gate for the current session.
